The `public contributions` by users in Ethiopia on `2026/10/17 10:39 PM UTC`. This list contains users from `Ethiopia` and cities `Addis-ababa` `Dire-dawa` `Dire-dawa` `Hawassa` `Dessie`.

There are `138 countries` and `675 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/xiv3r/top-github-users-ranking).

There are `986 users`  in Ethiopia. You need at least `20 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Ethiopia GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/ethiopia.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
